# Commands · P · part 3

1,423 entries, pbcopy to perm-audit. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **pbcopy**: Copy plan file content or path to clipboard ([MH4GF/shared-config](https://github.com/MH4GF/shared-config/blob/HEAD/.claude-plugins/plan-tools/commands/pbcopy.md) · ★2)
- **pbi-analyse**: Plan a build or migration before any code is written ([futureanalytics-io/ai-pbi-workbench](https://github.com/futureanalytics-io/ai-pbi-workbench/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pbi-analyse.md))
- **pbi-assign**: Assign or reassign tasks based on intelligent allocation ([gonzalezpazmonica/savia](https://github.com/gonzalezpazmonica/savia/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pbi-assign.md) · ★49)
- **pbi-audit**: Run a whole-model health audit - static BPA plus dynamic VertiPaq/Server-Timings ([futureanalytics-io/ai-pbi-workbench](https://github.com/futureanalytics-io/ai-pbi-workbench/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pbi-audit.md))
- **pbi-calc-group**: Author a calculation group (time intelligence, currency, or format switching) as TMDL, collapsing repeated measure variants into reusable calculation items. ([TheLobbi/Claude-m](https://github.com/TheLobbi/Claude-m/blob/HEAD/powerbi-fabric/commands/pbi-calc-group.md) · ★19)
- **pbi-card**: Build a KPI card for a metric ([futureanalytics-io/ai-pbi-workbench](https://github.com/futureanalytics-io/ai-pbi-workbench/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pbi-card.md))
- **pbi-commit**: Persist the current Desktop state to disk (Ctrl+S) and create one clean git commit of the project folder. Usage: /pbi-commit [message] ([CPobs/PBI-assistance](https://github.com/CPobs/PBI-assistance/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pbi-commit.md) · ★1)
- **pbi-dashboard-create**: Create Power BI dashboards in Fabric with deterministic setup and verification checks. ([TheLobbi/Claude-m](https://github.com/TheLobbi/Claude-m/blob/HEAD/powerbi-fabric/commands/pbi-dashboard-create.md) · ★19)
- **pbi-dataflow**: Scaffold a Fabric Dataflow Gen2 Power Query M definition for common source types with publishing guidance ([TheLobbi/Claude-m](https://github.com/TheLobbi/Claude-m/blob/HEAD/powerbi-fabric/commands/pbi-dataflow.md) · ★19)
- **pbi-dataset-refresh**: Generate TypeScript code to trigger a Power BI dataset refresh and poll for completion status via the REST API. ([TheLobbi/Claude-m](https://github.com/TheLobbi/Claude-m/blob/HEAD/powerbi-fabric/commands/pbi-dataset-refresh.md) · ★19)
- **pbi-dax**: Generate a DAX measure or pattern for a specific business requirement ([JosiahSiegel/claude-plugin-marketplace](https://github.com/JosiahSiegel/claude-plugin-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/powerbi-master/commands/pbi-dax.md) · ★63)

- **pbi-decompose**: Decompose a PBI into granular technical tasks ([gonzalezpazmonica/savia](https://github.com/gonzalezpazmonica/savia/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/pbi-decompose.md) · ★49)

([TheLobbi/Claude-m](https://github.com/TheLobbi/Claude-m/blob/HEAD/powerbi-fabric/commands/pbi-deploy-pipeline.md) · ★19)
